-/-- A zero-row DML statement need not publish an unchanged database. The
+/-- Updating identical non-key payload bytes does not dirty a SQLite table
+page. Assigning a rowid or indexed column still performs structural writes. -/
+def updatePayloadOnly (state : Core.State) (table : String)
+    (assignments : Array (String × Expr)) : Bool :=
+  match state.database.findTable? (CoreSQL.canonical table) with
+  | none => false
+  | some stored => assignments.all fun assignment =>
+      match stored.schema.column? assignment.1 with
+      | none => false
+      | some column => column != stored.schema.primaryKey &&
+          !(stored.indexes.any fun index => index.column == column)
+
+/-- Zero-row DML and identical non-key payload UPDATEs need not publish an
+unchanged database. The
 content comparison is necessary even with zero reported changes: AUTOINCREMENT
 may still initialize or advance sqlite_sequence. Transaction controls are not

+        updatePayloadOnly current table assignments) &&
+        current.database.contents == next.database.contents

+/-- Preserve quoted-token diagnostics before name binding discards that
+syntax marker. The resolver supplies its actual visible column set. -/
+def nameErrorFuel (bound : Option String → String → Bool) : Nat → Expr → Option String
+  | 0, _ => none
+  | fuel+1, expression =>
+      let check := nameErrorFuel bound fuel
+      if let some name := quotedIdentifier? expression then
+        if bound none name then none
+        else some ("no such column: \"" ++ name ++ "\" - should this be a string literal in single-quotes?")
+      else match expression with
+      | .column qualifier name =>
+          if bound qualifier name then none
+          else some ("no such column: " ++ (qualifier.map (· ++ ".") |>.getD "") ++ name)
+      | .call _ arguments _ => arguments.toList.findSome? check
+      | .unary _ argument | .isNull argument _ | .collate argument _ |
+          .inSubquery argument _ _ => check argument
+      | .binary _ left right => (check left).orElse (fun _ => check right)
+      | .inList argument values _ _ => (check argument).orElse (fun _ => values.toList.findSome? check)
+      | .case_ base branches otherwise =>
+          (base.bind check).orElse (fun _ =>
+            (branches.toList.findSome? (fun pair => (check pair.1).orElse (fun _ => check pair.2))).orElse
+              (fun _ => otherwise.bind check))
+      | _ => none
+
+def nameError (bound : Option String → String → Bool) (expression : Expr) : Option String :=
+  let expression := prepare expression
+  nameErrorFuel bound expression.height expression
+
+theorem nameError_collapsed (bound : Option String → String → Bool) (expression : Expr)
+    (collapsed : prepare expression = .literal (.integer 0)) : nameError bound expression = none := by
+  simp [nameError, collapsed, nameErrorFuel, quotedIdentifier?, Expr.height]
+

     (left right : Expr) (message : String)
     (failed : admissionErrorFuel fuel left = some message) :

+private def expectSQLiteJsonAccess : IO Unit := do
+  -- 5,343 pinned C controls; the original component diagnostics and all inputs
+  -- are retained separately from production executable evaluations.
+  let cases := jsonAccessControls0 ++ jsonAccessControls1 ++ jsonAccessControls2 ++ jsonAccessControls3 ++ jsonAccessControls4 ++ jsonAccessControls5 ++ jsonAccessControls6 ++ jsonAccessControls7 ++ jsonAccessControls8
+  for (sql, expected, expectedError) in cases do
+    match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty sql with
+    | .error message => unless expectedError == some message do fail s!"JSON access error differs: {sql}: {message}"
+    | .ok (_, results) =>
+        if expectedError.isSome then fail s!"JSON access unexpectedly succeeded: {sql}"
+        let wanted ← match expected.mapM SQLSurface.value with
+          | .ok row => pure row
+          | .error message => fail message
+        unless (results.getLast?).map (·.rows) == some [wanted] do
+          fail s!"JSON access differs from C: {sql}"
+
+private def expectSQLiteByteDiagnostics : IO Unit := do
+  -- All 65,536 two-byte strings exercise lossless diagnostic transport,
+  -- including malformed UTF-8 and embedded NUL. This is a component control.
+  for high in [0:256] do
+    for low in [0:256] do
+      let data := #[UInt8.ofNat high, UInt8.ofNat low]
+      unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes (SQLite.Diagnostic.ofBytes data) == data do
+        fail "diagnostic byte roundtrip"
+  let marker := SQLite.Diagnostic.envelope.toUTF8.data
+  unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes (SQLite.Diagnostic.ofBytes marker) == marker do
+    fail "diagnostic envelope collision"
+  for fn in ["json_type", "json_array_length"] do
+    let sql := "SELECT " ++ fn ++ "('0',CAST(X'24ff' AS TEXT));"
+    match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty sql with
+    | .error message =>
+        unless SQLite.Diagnostic.bytes message == "bad JSON path: '$".toUTF8.data ++ #[255,39] do
+          fail "JSON path diagnostic lost invalid UTF-8"
+    | .ok _ => fail "invalid JSON path was admitted"
+
+private def expectSQLiteIdenticalUpdates : IO Unit := do
+  let cases : List (String × String × Bool) := [
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE UNIQUE INDEX ix ON t(v);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=NULL WHERE id=1;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET w=w;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET id=id;", false),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v RETURNING id;", true),
+    ("CREATE TABLE t(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,v,w);INSERT INTO t VALUES(1,NULL,1),(2,2,2);CREATE INDEX ix ON t(w);", "UPDATE t SET v=v WHERE 0;", true)]
+  for (setup, sql, unchanged) in cases do
+    let (before, _) ← match CoreSQL.execute Core.State.empty setup with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    let statements ← match CoreTokenGrammar.parseStatements sql with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    let statement ← match statements[0]? with
+      | some statement => pure statement | none => fail "missing UPDATE statement"
+    let (after, results) ← match CoreSQL.executeStatement before statement with
+      | .ok x => pure x | .error message => fail message
+    unless CoreFileEngine.dataWriteNoop before after statement results == unchanged do
+      fail s!"identical UPDATE publication disagrees with C: {setup} {sql}"
+

NEW FILE SQLeanite/SQLite/JsonAccess.lean
import SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonValid
import SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic

/-! Read-only JSON navigation. Text is parsed to an internal binary tree;
external JSONB retains its shallow admission and operation-local checks. -/
namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonAccess
open JsonValid

def require (message : String) (value : Option α) : Except String α :=
  match value with | some value => .ok value | none => .error message

def slice (input : Array UInt8) (start finish : Nat) : Array UInt8 :=
  input.extract start finish

def node (kind : Nat) (data : Array UInt8) : Array UInt8 :=
  let width := if data.size < 12 then 0 else if data.size < 256 then 1
    else if data.size < 65536 then 2 else 4
  let tag := if width == 0 then data.size else if width == 1 then 12 else if width == 2 then 13 else 14
  #[UInt8.ofNat (tag*16+kind)] ++
    (List.range width).toArray.map (fun i => UInt8.ofNat (data.size / 256^(width-i-1))) ++ data

structure Encoded where
  next : Nat
  data : Array UInt8

def encodedString (input : Array UInt8) (start finish : Nat) : Array UInt8 :=
  let data := slice input start finish
  node (if data.any (fun c => c.toNat == 92) then 9 else 10) data

mutual
  def encode (input : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Option Encoded
    | 0, _ => none
    | fuel+1, pos => do
        let pos := (skip input (input.size+1) pos false).1
        let c := byte input pos
        if c == 123 || c == 91 then
          let children ← encodeMembers input fuel (c == 123) (pos+1) #[]
          return { children with data := node (if c == 123 then 12 else 11) children.data }
        if c == 34 || c == 39 then
          let parsed ← string input c (input.size+1) (pos+1) (c == 39)
          return ⟨parsed.next, encodedString input (pos+1) (parsed.next-1)⟩
        let parsed ← if digit c || [43,45,46].contains c then number input pos else atom input pos
        let data := slice input pos parsed.next
        let name := data.toList.map (fun b => lower b.toNat)
        let kind := if name == [116,114,117,101] then 1
          else if name == [102,97,108,115,101] then 2
          else if name == [110,117,108,108] || name.contains 110 && !name.contains 105 then 0
          else if name.contains 105 then 5
          else if name.contains 120 then 4
          else if name.contains 46 || name.contains 101 then 5 else 3
        return ⟨parsed.next, node kind (if kind ≤ 2 then #[] else data)⟩
  def encodeMembers (input : Array UInt8) : Nat → Bool → Nat → Array UInt8 → Option Encoded
    | 0, _, _, _ => none
    | fuel+1, object, pos, collected => do
        let pos := (skip input (input.size+1) pos false).1
        let close := if object then 125 else 93
        if byte input pos == close then return ⟨pos+1, collected⟩
        let (pos, collected) ← if object then do
            let c := byte input pos
            let (finish, data) ← if c == 34 || c == 39 then do
                let parsed ← string input c (input.size+1) (pos+1) (c == 39)
                pure (parsed.next, encodedString input (pos+1) (parsed.next-1))
              else do
                let parsed ← identifier input pos
                pure (parsed.next, encodedString input pos parsed.next)
            let colon := (skip input (input.size+1) finish false).1
            unless byte input colon == 58 do none
            pure (colon+1, collected ++ data)
          else pure (pos, collected)
        let child ← encode input fuel pos
        let after := (skip input (input.size+1) child.next false).1
        let collected := collected ++ child.data
        if byte input after == close then return ⟨after+1, collected⟩
        unless byte input after == 44 do none
        encodeMembers input fuel object (after+1) collected
end

def load (value : Value) : Except String (Option (Array UInt8)) := do
  if Text.isNull value then return none
  let input := Text.bytes value
  if (match value with | .blob _ => blobLooks input | _ => false) then return some input
  unless textValid input true do throw "malformed JSON"
  match encode input (4*input.size+4) 0 with
  | some parsed => return some parsed.data
  | none => throw "malformed JSON"

def arrayCountLoop (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat
  | 0, _, count => count
  | fuel+1, pos, count =>
      if pos ≥ finish then count else
      match payload input pos with
      | none => count+1
      | some (header, size) => arrayCountLoop input finish fuel (pos+header+size) (count+1)

def arrayCount (input : Array UInt8) (pos : Nat) : Nat :=
  match payload input pos with
  | none => 0
  | some (header, size) => arrayCountLoop input (pos+header+size) (input.size+1) (pos+header) 0

def hexInt (c : Nat) : Nat := (c + 9*((c/64)%2)) % 16
def hex4 (input : Array UInt8) (pos : Nat) : Nat :=
  (List.range 4).foldl (fun n i => n*16 + hexInt (byte input (pos+i))) 0

/-- utf.c:sqlite3Utf8ReadLimited decodes at most four bytes without scalar
validation. This differs intentionally from SQL unicode(). -/
def utf8 (input : Array UInt8) (pos finish : Nat) : Nat × Nat := Id.run do
  if pos ≥ finish then return (0, pos)
  let c := byte input pos
  if c < 192 then return (c, pos+1)
  let mut value := if c < 224 then c%32 else if c < 240 then c%16
    else if c < 248 then c%8 else if c < 252 then c%4 else if c < 254 then c%2 else 0
  let mut next := pos+1
  for _ in [0:3] do
    if next < finish && byte input next / 64 == 2 then
      value := value*64 + byte input next % 64
      next := next+1
    else break
  return (value, next)

def continuationEnd (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat
  | 0, pos => pos
  | fuel+1, pos =>
      if pos+1 ≥ finish || byte input pos != 92 then pos
      else if byte input (pos+1) == 10 then continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos+2)
      else if byte input (pos+1) == 13 then
        continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos + if pos+2 < finish && byte input (pos+2) == 10 then 3 else 2)
      else if pos+4 ≤ finish && lineSeparator input (pos+1) then continuationEnd input finish fuel (pos+4)
      else pos

def unescape (input : Array UInt8) (finish : Nat) : Nat → Nat → Nat × Nat
  | 0, _ => (629145, finish)
  | fuel+1, pos =>
      if pos+1 ≥ finish then (629145, finish) else
      let c := byte input (pos+1)
      if c == 117 then
        if pos+6 > finish then (629145, finish) else
        let value := hex4 input (pos+2)
        let low := hex4 input (pos+8)
        if value / 1024 == 54 && pos+12 ≤ finish && byte input (pos+6) == 92 &&
            byte input (pos+7) == 117 && low / 1024 == 55 then
          ((value%1024)*1024 + low%1024 + 65536, pos+12)
        else (value, pos+6)
      else if c == 120 then
        if pos+4 > finish then (629145, finish)
        else (hexInt (byte input (pos+2))*16 + hexInt (byte input (pos+3)), pos+4)
      else if c == 48 then
        (if pos+2 < finish && digit (byte input (pos+2)) then 629145 else 0, pos+2)
      else if [34,39,47,92].contains c then (c, pos+2)
      else match [(98,8),(102,12),(110,10),(114,13),(116,9),(118,11)].find? (fun p => p.1 == c) with
        | some p => (p.2, pos+2)
        | none =>
            let next := continuationEnd input finish (input.size+1) pos
            if next == pos then (629145, pos+2)
            else if next ≥ finish then (0, next)
            else if byte input next == 92 then unescape input finish fuel next
            else utf8 input next finish

def labelChar (input : Array UInt8) (pos finish : Nat) (raw : Bool) : Nat × Nat :=
  if pos ≥ finish then (0, pos)
  else if !raw && byte input pos == 92 then unescape input finish (input.size+1) pos
  else utf8 input pos finish

def labelEqualLoop (left right : Array UInt8) (rawLeft rawRight : Bool) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Bool
  | 0, _, _ => false
  | fuel+1, lpos, rpos =>
      let l := labelChar left lpos left.size rawLeft
      let r := labelChar right rpos right.size rawRight
      l.1 == r.1 && (l.1 == 0 || labelEqualLoop left right rawLeft rawRight fuel l.2 r.2)

def labelEqual (left right : Array UInt8) (rawLeft rawRight : Bool) : Bool :=
  if rawLeft && rawRight then left == right
  else labelEqualLoop left right rawLeft rawRight (left.size+right.size+2) 0 0

def quotedEnd (path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Option Nat
  | 0, _ => none
  | fuel+1, pos =>
      if byte path pos == 0 then none
      else if byte path pos == 34 then some pos
      else quotedEnd path fuel (pos + if byte path pos == 92 && byte path (pos+1) != 0 then 2 else 1)

def digits (path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat × Nat
  | 0, pos, value => (pos, value)
  | fuel+1, pos, value =>
      if digit (byte path pos) then
        digits path fuel (pos+1) (if value < 4294967295 then value*10 + byte path pos - 48 else value)
      else (pos, value)

def pathError (path : Array UInt8) : String :=
  let data := (path.toList.takeWhile (fun c => c.toNat != 0)).flatMap
    (fun c => if c.toNat == 39 then [c,c] else [c])
  Diagnostic.ofBytes ("bad JSON path: '".toUTF8.data ++ data.toArray ++ #[39])

mutual
  def lookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
    | 0, _, _, _ => .error "JSON path too deep"
    | fuel+1, depth, root, pos => do
        let c := byte path pos
        if c == 0 then return some root
        if c == 46 then
          let (key, after, raw) ← if byte path (pos+1) == 34 then do
              let finish ← require (pathError path) (quotedEnd path (path.size+1) (pos+2))
              let key := slice path (pos+2) finish
              pure (key, finish+1, !key.any (fun c => c.toNat == 92))
            else do
              let finish := span path (fun c => c != 0 && c != 46 && c != 91) (path.size+1) (pos+1)
              if finish == pos+1 then throw (pathError path)
              pure (slice path (pos+1) finish, finish, true)
          if byte input root % 16 != 12 then return none
          let (header, size) := (payload input root).getD (0,0)
          objectLookup input path fuel depth (root+header) (root+header+size) key raw after
        else if c == 91 then
          if byte input root % 16 != 11 then return none
          let (header, size) := (payload input root).getD (0,0)
          let (finish, index) := digits path (path.size+1) (pos+1) 0
          let (finish, index) ← if finish > pos+1 && byte path finish == 93 then pure (finish, index)
            else if byte path (pos+1) == 35 then do
              let count := arrayCount input root
              if byte path (pos+2) == 45 && digit (byte path (pos+3)) then
                let (finish, back) := digits path (path.size+1) (pos+3) 0
                if back > count then return none
                if byte path finish != 93 then throw (pathError path)
                pure (finish, count-back)
              else
                if byte path (pos+2) != 93 then throw (pathError path)
                pure (pos+2, count)
            else throw (pathError path)
          arrayLookup input path fuel depth (root+header) (root+header+size) index (finish+1)
        else throw (pathError path)
  def objectLookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Array UInt8 → Bool → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
    | 0, _, _, _, _, _, _ => .error "malformed JSON"
    | fuel+1, depth, pos, finish, key, raw, after => do
        if pos == finish then return none
        if pos > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
        let kind := byte input pos % 16
        unless 7 ≤ kind && kind ≤ 10 do throw "malformed JSON"
        let (header, size) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input pos)
        let value := pos+header+size
        if value ≥ finish then throw "malformed JSON"
        if byte input value % 16 > 12 then throw "malformed JSON"
        let (vheader, vsize) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input value)
        if labelEqual key (slice input (pos+header) value) raw (kind == 7 || kind == 10) then
          if value+vheader+vsize > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
          if depth+1 ≥ 1000 then throw "JSON path too deep"
          lookup input path fuel (depth+1) value after
        else objectLookup input path fuel depth (value+vheader+vsize) finish key raw after
  def arrayLookup (input path : Array UInt8) : Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Nat → Except String (Option Nat)
    | 0, _, _, _, _, _ => .error "malformed JSON"
    | fuel+1, depth, pos, finish, index, after => do
        if pos == finish then return none
        if pos > finish then throw "malformed JSON"
        if index == 0 then
          if depth+1 ≥ 1000 then throw "JSON path too deep"
          lookup input path fuel (depth+1) pos after
        else
          let (header, size) ← require "malformed JSON" (payload input pos)
          arrayLookup input path fuel depth (pos+header+size) finish (index-1) after
end

def select (input : Array UInt8) (path : Option Value) : Except String (Option Nat) := do
  match path with
  | none => return some 0
  | some .null => return none
  | some value =>
      let path := Text.bytes value
      unless byte path 0 == 36 do throw (pathError path)
      lookup input path (4*(input.size+path.size)+4) 0 0 1

def typeName (kind : Nat) : String :=
  ["null","true","false","integer","integer","real","real","text","text","text","text","array","object"].getD kind ""

def evaluateType (value : Value) (path : Option Value := none) : Except String Value := do
  let some input ← load value | return .null
  let some pos ← select input path | return .null
  return .text (typeName (byte input pos % 16))

def evaluateLength (value : Value) (path : Option Value := none) : Except String Value := do
  let some input ← load value | return .null
  let some pos ← select input path | return .null
  return .integer (if byte input pos % 16 == 11 then arrayCount input pos else 0)

theorem load_null : load .null = .ok none := by
  rfl

theorem evaluateType_null (path : Option Value) : evaluateType .null path = .ok .null := by
  rfl

theorem evaluateLength_null (path : Option Value) : evaluateLength .null path = .ok .null := by
  rfl

/-- Missing paths produce SQL NULL even when the document is non-NULL. -/
theorem evaluateType_missing (value : Value) (path : Option Value) (input : Array UInt8)
    (loaded : load value = .ok (some input)) (missing : select input path = .ok none) :
    evaluateType value path = .ok .null := by
  simp [evaluateType, loaded, missing, bind, Except.bind, pure, Except.pure]

/-- Counting does not require recursive validation of external JSONB. -/
theorem arrayCountLoop_truncated (input : Array UInt8) (finish fuel pos count : Nat)
    (inside : pos < finish) (truncated : payload input pos = none) :
    arrayCountLoop input finish (fuel+1) pos count = count+1 := by
  simp [arrayCountLoop, Nat.not_le_of_lt inside, truncated]

end SQLeanite.SQLite.JsonAccess

NEW FILE SQLeanite/SQLite/Diagnostic.lean
import SQLeanite.SQLite.Text

/-! Lossless byte diagnostics carried through the existing Except String API.
UTF-8 messages remain plain strings. Non-UTF-8 messages use a reserved NUL-led
hex envelope until the executable writes stderr bytes. This encodes only the
message supplied by the failing operation; it does not inspect SQL or results. -/
namespace SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic

def envelope : String := "\x00SQLeanite/error-bytes/"

def ofBytes (data : Array UInt8) : String :=
  match String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ with
  | some text => if text.startsWith envelope then envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data else text
  | none => envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data

def bytes (message : String) : Array UInt8 :=
  if message.startsWith envelope then
    let encoded := (message.drop envelope.length).toString.toList.map Char.toNat
    (Text.unhexCodes [] encoded #[]).getD message.toUTF8.data
  else message.toUTF8.data

theorem ofBytes_utf8 (data : Array UInt8) (message : String)
    (valid : String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ = some message)
    (ordinary : message.startsWith envelope = false) : ofBytes data = message := by
  simp [ofBytes, valid, ordinary]

theorem bytes_ordinary (message : String)
    (ordinary : message.startsWith envelope = false) : bytes message = message.toUTF8.data := by
  simp [bytes, ordinary]

theorem ofBytes_nonutf8 (data : Array UInt8)
    (invalid : String.fromUTF8? ⟨data⟩ = none) : ofBytes data = envelope ++ Text.hexBytes data := by
  simp [ofBytes, invalid]

end SQLeanite.SQLite.Diagnostic
